<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-91007B07-FD0E-4150-A5EA-C49F1D222022'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $840$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \text { of } 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 840} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 210 \\\\
&= 630
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$630$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \text { of } 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 840} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 210 \\\\
&= 630
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$630$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F5880817-921D-447F-A99E-9260020AF176'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Tom uses $750 \ \text{g}$ of sugar from a $2.5 \ \text{kg}$ packet. How many kilograms of sugar are left? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sugar left}           &= \text{Total sugar} - \text{Sugar used by Tom} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 \ \text{kg} - 750 \ \text{g} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 \ \text{kg} - \dfrac {750} {1000} \ \text{kg} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 - \dfrac {750} {1000} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 - 0.75 \\\\
                            &= 1.75 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.75 \ \text{kg}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sugar left}           &= \text{Total sugar} - \text{Sugar used by Tom} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 \ \text{kg} - 750 \ \text{g} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 \ \text{kg} - \dfrac {750} {1000} \ \text{kg} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 - \dfrac {750} {1000} \\\\
                            &= 2.5 - 0.75 \\\\
                            &= 1.75 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1.75 \ \text{kg}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Given that Tom eats $\dfrac{3}{5}$ of a cake and his friend, Jerry, eats $\dfrac{1}{3}$ of the cake, what fraction of the cake is left? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cake left}   &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {3} {5} + \dfrac {1} {3} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {3 \times 3} {5 \times 3} + \dfrac {1 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {9} {15} + \dfrac {5} {15} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {9 + 5} {15} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 15} {1 \times 15} - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15} - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 - 14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned} 
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {15}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cake left}   &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {3} {5} + \dfrac {1} {3} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {3 \times 3} {5 \times 3} + \dfrac {1 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {9} {15} + \dfrac {5} {15} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \left( \dfrac {9 + 5} {15} \right) \\\\
                    &= 1 - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 15} {1 \times 15} - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15} - \dfrac {14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 - 14} {15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned} 
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {15}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Given that Tom’s piece of cake weighed $150 \ \text{grams}$, what was the weight of the whole cake? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Weight of cake}      &= \dfrac {5} {3} \times 150 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 150} {3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 50} {3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {3} \times 50} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                            &= 5 \times 50 \\\\
                            &= 250 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$250 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Weight of cake}      &= \dfrac {5} {3} \times 150 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 150} {3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 50} {3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {3} \times 50} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                            &= 5 \times 50 \\\\
                            &= 250 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$250 \ \text {g}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BFD242F4-6219-48DB-A84D-A172EAB41CB9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write the following events in order, with the **most** probable (i.e. **most** likely) first and the **least** probable 
(i.e. **least** likely) last. Give your answer as a sequence of letters, e.g. BACED.

-  Being born on a Wednesday;
-  A meteorite falls on the College tomorrow;
-  Rolling a $4$ with a fair die;
-  Obtaining a head when a fair coin is tossed;
-  Obtaining an even number when two odd numbers are multiplied together. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

| P(Obtaining a head when a fair coin is tossed)    | $\dfrac {1} {2}$  | D     |
|:---:  |:---:  |:---:  |
| P(Rolling a 4 with a fair die)    | $\dfrac {1} {6}$  | C     |
| P(Being born on a Wednesday)  | $\dfrac {1} {7}$  | A     |
| p(A meteorite falls on the College tomorrow)  |   | B     |
| P(Obtaining an even number when two odd numbers are multiplied together)  | $0$   | E     |

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

DCABE

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>| P(Obtaining a head when a fair coin is tossed)    | $\dfrac {1} {2}$  | D     |
|:---:  |:---:  |:---:  |
| P(Rolling a 4 with a fair die)    | $\dfrac {1} {6}$  | C     |
| P(Being born on a Wednesday)  | $\dfrac {1} {7}$  | A     |
| p(A meteorite falls on the College tomorrow)  |   | B     |
| P(Obtaining an even number when two odd numbers are multiplied together)  | $0$   | E     |
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>DCABE
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Rosa takes $37 \ \text{minutes}$ to travel to work each morning. She leaves home at $8.15 \ \text{a.m.}$ What time does she arrive at work? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Arrival Time} &= 8{:}15 + 37 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                     &= 8{:}52 \ \text {am}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8{:}52 \ \text {am}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Arrival Time} &= 8{:}15 + 37 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                     &= 8{:}52 \ \text {am}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8{:}52 \ \text {am}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The journey home in the evening takes the same time. She arrives home at $6.23 \ \text{p.m.}$. At what time did she leave work? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {She left} &= 6{:}23 - 37 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                 &= 5{:}46 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5{:}46 \ \text {pm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How much will it cost to buy $10 \ \text{metres}$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 10 m curtain material}      &= 10 \times 10.2 \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 102
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 102$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 10 m curtain material}      &= 10 \times 10.2 \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 102
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 102$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Mr Rutter buys a length of material costing $\pounds 25.50$.
How many metres does he buy?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Length}                                 &= \dfrac {25.5} {10.2} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {255} {102} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5 \times 51} {2 \times 51} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {51}} {2 \times \cancel {51}} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
                                              &= 2.5 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.5 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Length}                                 &= \dfrac {25.5} {10.2} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {255} {102} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5 \times 51} {2 \times 51} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {51}} {2 \times \cancel {51}} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {5} {2} \\\\
                                              &= 2.5 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.5 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F139E74C-7678-4CC8-B3F7-4D88629C12D1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The distance from Calais to Paris is $320 \ \text{km}$.
$5 \ \text{miles}$ is approximately $8 \ \text{kilometres}$.
 
Calculate the approximate distance in miles from Calais to Paris.  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance from Calais to Paris in miles}    &= 320 \ \text{km} \\\\
                                                 &= 8 \ \text{km} \times 40 \\\\
                                                 &= 5 \ \text{miles} \times 40 \\\\
                                                 &= 200 \ \text{miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$200 \ \text {miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance from Calais to Paris in miles}    &= 320 \ \text{km} \\\\
                                                 &= 8 \ \text{km} \times 40 \\\\
                                                 &= 5 \ \text{miles} \times 40 \\\\
                                                 &= 200 \ \text{miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$200 \ \text {miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CB7EF112-E190-4553-823D-BE6A00EE6C10'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Shortcrust pastry is made using flour and fat in the ratio $2 : 1$.

How many grams of flour are needed to make $900 \ \text{grams}$ of shortcrust pastry?  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Flour needed}              &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 900 \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {2 \times 900} {3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {2 \times 300 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {2 \times 300 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \times 300  \\\\
                                 &= 600 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Let the quantity of flour needed $= a$

Quantity of fat needed $= 900 - a$

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{grams} : 2 \ \text{grams}                     &= (900 - a) \ \text{grams} : a \ \text{grams} \\\\
\dfrac{1} {2}                                   &= \dfrac {900 - a} {a} \\\\
a                                               &= (900 - a) \times 2 \\\\
a                                               &= 1800 - 2a \\\\
a + 2a                                          &= 1800 \\\\
3a                                              &= 1800 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {1800} {3} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {3 \times 600} {3} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 600} {\cancel 3} \\\\
a                                               &= 600 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$

Flour $= 600 \ \text{g}$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using whole to part ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{pastry}                       &:& \text{flour} \\\\
= & 2 + 1                               &:& 2 \\\\
= & 3                                   &:& 2 \\\\
= & 3 \times 300                        &:& 2 \times 300 \\\\
= & 900 \ \text{g}                      &:& 600 \ \text{g}
\end{array}
$

Flour $= 600 \ \text{g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$600 \ \text{g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

                                 &= \dfrac {2 \times 300 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                                 &= 2 \times 300  \\\\
                                 &= 600 \ \text{g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2654C266-6341-484A-B3AF-C6FDC62EF599'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Three bus services stop at my bus stop. Service $A$ departs every $3 \ \text{minutes}$, Service $B$ every $5 \ \text{minutes}$ 
and Service $C$ every $8 \ \text{minutes}$s.

If all three services leave my stop at $10.00 \ \text{a.m.}$, at what time will they next leave my stop together? (Assume that all three services always run on time). 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$2.00 \ \text{a.m.}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.00 \ \text{a.m.}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.00 \ \text{a.m.}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.00 \ \text{a.m.}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Four rectangles each of length $27 \ \text{cm}$ and width $13 \ \text{cm}$ are arranged to form the square shape shown below (the diagram is not drawn to scale)

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


Find: 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

the perimeter of this square shape.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of square} = 4 \times \text{side}$


As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of square} &= 4 \times 40 \\\\
                            &= 160 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$160 \ \text {cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_expanded_perimeter_square
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter of square} &= 4 \times 40 \\\\
                            &= 160 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$160 \ \text {cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

the area of the shaded inner square. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$


Let the length of shaded inner square $= l$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {l}       &= 40 - (2 \times 13) \\\\
\text {l}       &= 40 - 26 \\\\
\text {l}       &= 14
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $l$ to find area of shaded inner square.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area}           &= 14 \times 14 \\\\
                       &= 196 \ \text {cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$196 \ \text {cm} ^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                       &= 196 \ \text {cm} ^2

$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Calculate the area of $ABCD$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Height of triangle ABC}   &= 5 - 3 \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Base of triangle ABC}     &= 6 - 0 \\\\
                                &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area of triangle ABC}     &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text{Height of triangle ABC} \times \text{Base of triangle ABC} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 2 \times 6 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 6} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                                &= 6 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2 \\\\
\text{Area of kite ABCD}        &= 2 \times \text{Area of triangle ABC} \\\\
                                &= 2 \times 6 \\\\
                                &= 12 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Height of triangle ABC}   &= 5 - 3 \\\\
                                &= 2 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Base of triangle ABC}     &= 6 - 0 \\\\
                                &= 6 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area of triangle ABC}     &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text{Height of triangle ABC} \times \text{Base of triangle ABC} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 2 \times 6 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 6} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                                &= 6 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2 \\\\
\text{Area of kite ABCD}        &= 2 \times \text{Area of triangle ABC} \\\\
                                &= 2 \times 6 \\\\
                                &= 12 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C5C3BAF3-583A-4F15-9F82-00938336CDCD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

My age is a multiple of $7$. Next year it will be a multiple of $5$. I am more than $20$ years old, but less than $80$. How old am I? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

We can easily infer their values by using trial and error. Let's find the number which meets the following critiera below.

- Their age lies between $20$ and $80$.
- Their age is a multiple of $7$.
- Their age next year is a multiple of $5$.
<br>

| Age       |  Age + $1$       | Multiple of $5$  |
|:---------:|:----------------:|:----------------:|
|   $21$    |       $22$       |        No        |
|   $28$    |       $29$       |        No        |
|   $35$    |       $36$       |        No        |
|   $42$    |       $43$       |        No        |
|   $49$    |       $50$       |       Yes        |
|   $56$    |       $57$       |        No        |
|   $63$    |       $64$       |        No        |
|   $70$    |       $71$       |        No        |
|   $77$    |       $78$       |        No        |

Age $= 49 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$49 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>We can easily infer their values by using trial and error. Let's find the number which meets the following critiera below.
<emptyline>
- Their age lies between $20$ and $80$.
- Their age is a multiple of $7$.
- Their age next year is a multiple of $5$.
<br>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BD44A344-8EDA-4BCD-978F-FE8E11154BA8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A piece of wire $30 \ \text{cm}$ long is bent into the shape of a rectangle. 
If the rectangle is twice as long as it is wide, what is its area?  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Perimeter of rectangle} = 2 \times (\text{length} + \text{width})$


ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


Let the width of the rectangle $= a$

Length of the rectangle $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (2a + a)                         &= 30 \\\\
2 \times 3a                               &= 30 \\\\
6a                                        &= 30 \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
a                                         &= 5 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Width of the rectangle $= 5 \ \text {cm}$

Length of the rectangle $= 10 \ \text {cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the rectangle}        &= 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                    &= 50 \ \text {cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$50 \ \text{cm} ^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_expanded_perimeter_rectangle
<emptyline>
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded
<emptyline>
Let the width of the rectangle $= a$
<emptyline>
Length of the rectangle $= 2a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (2a + a)                         &= 30 \\\\
2 \times 3a                               &= 30 \\\\
6a                                        &= 30 \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {6} \\\\
a                                         &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
a                                         &= 5 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Width of the rectangle $= 5 \ \text {cm}$
<emptyline>
Length of the rectangle $= 10 \ \text {cm}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of the rectangle}        &= 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                    &= 50 \ \text {cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$50 \ \text{cm} ^2$
</code></pre>
</div>

                      &= 4 \ \text{min}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='workings'>

$

                        &= 16 \ \text{min}
\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Peter walked at $4\ \text{km/h}$. 

How far is it around the school field? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance travelled by Peter}    &= \text{Speed} \times \text{Time} \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times (15 \ \text{min}) \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times (\dfrac {15} {60} \ \text{h}) \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times \dfrac {15} {60} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times 15} {60} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times 15} {4 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times \cancel {15}} {\cancel 4 \times \cancel {15}} \\\\
                                      &= 1 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1 \ \text{km}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note

\text{Distance travelled by Peter}    &= \text{Speed} \times \text{Time} \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times (15 \ \text{min}) \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times (\dfrac {15} {60} \ \text{h}) \\\\
                                      &= 4 \times \dfrac {15} {60} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times 15} {60} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {4 \times 15} {4 \times 15} \\\\
                                      &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times \cancel {15}} {\cancel 4 \times \cancel {15}} \\\\
                                      &= 1 \ \text{km}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Two numbers are *clotted* by multiplying them together and then writing the answer backwards.

For example, $6$ and $7$ are *clotted* to give $24$ because $6 \times 7 = 42$ and $42$ written backwards is $24$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the result of *clotting* $8$ and $9$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 9\\\\
&= 72 
\end{aligned}
$

$72$ written backward $= 27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times 9\\\\
&= 72 
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$72$ written backward $= 27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

With which number must 4 be *clotted* with to give $42$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$42$ backwards = $24$

let the number = a

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times \text a &= 24 \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel 4}{\cancel 4} \\\\
\text a          &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$42$ backwards = $24$
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times \text a &= 24 \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
\text a          &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel 4}{\cancel 4} \\\\
\text a          &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find two whole numbers which *clot* to give $53$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$53$ backwards = $35$

let the numbers= a, b

$
\begin{aligned}
\text a \times \text b &= 35 \\\\
7 \times 5             &= 35
\end{aligned}
$
